DART is a tool that helps to bundle the appropriate log files and diagnostic information that can be used for analyzing and debugging the Cisco Secure Client connections.
    dartcli [options]
    Default option is used if options are not specified.
    Options:
    -clearlogs

    -u, -udid
          Display global unique device identifier information.
    -ul, -udid_legacy
          Display unique device identifier(legacy) information.

          Include Legacy - Cisco AnyConnect Secure Mobility Client logs.
